# Page and interaction inventory

**Partial exploration.** Pending rows remain; this is a resumable checkpoint, not exhaustive coverage.

The first rows record actual interactions. Discovered-control rows preserve visible controls conservatively until their individual behavior is reconciled. A screenshot reference on a pending row proves visibility only, not activation. Repeated controls are not assumed equivalent.

| Status | Rows |
|---|---:|
| blocked | 127 |
| explored and captured | 130 |
| explored and duplicate | 10 |
| not executed | 222 |
| pending | 2512 |

Light was restored after the authorized Dark capture. No creation, deletion, invitation, cache clear, or account update was submitted.

## Remaining branches

- All report links and lower pages: Both collection pages and all 29 saved-question overviews opened; per-report nested controls and dashboard lower sections remain; query failures and rejected images are recorded separately.
- Model detail pages and source table references: All five model overviews, metadata and relationships inspected; per-model nested menus, editor controls and remaining source-table references still pending.
- Data reference pages: Database, Segments, Glossary, Assignments table/fields/questions and Course ID field captured; other table and field references and lower related-question list remain.
- Metric Explore and charts below fold: Overview lower charts, drill interactions and Explore remain.
- Remaining personal metrics: Other metric records not yet grouped by verified shared behavior.
- Trash pagination and archived details: 977 archived records; safe shared behavior and exceptions not yet verified.
- Global AI exploration: Chat with Metabot asks admin to connect a model; New-menu AI entry still needs separate reconciliation.
- Mobile and Dark parity: Only report collection/mobile filter and selected Dark forms captured; full inventory pass remains.
- Keyboard focus and hover controls: Complete focus/hover pass still pending.
- Lower shortcut reference panes: General, Dashboards and Questions panes extend below captured section.
